Full raw console log: ci-application build #6 (real, webhook-triggered)
==========================================================================
"Started by GitHub push by MaxTsyganov" - real push webhook, not a manual
trigger. Commit 429f5a8944e523bba85b2fb3ad5bb36a6ace952a ("fix: sign the
digest crane actually pushed, not Kaniko's prediction" - the fix commit
itself, tested against its own change).

Demonstrates the restructured Build/Scan/Push+Sign order for real: Kaniko
builds each service to a local tarball only (--no-push --tarPath, no
--destination push), Trivy scans that tarball and gates on fixable
CRITICAL findings BEFORE anything reaches ECR, then crane pushes the
already-scanned tarball, and Cosign signs the digest crane itself reported
pushing. Search this file for:
  "--no-push"                  - Kaniko's build command, all 3 services
  "=== SBOM for"                - Trivy scanning the local tarball
  "Pushing devops-"              - crane push, after the scan already passed
  "Signing 957905603240"         - Cosign signing crane's own reported digest
  "Finished: SUCCESS"            - end of build

ECR registry auth tokens never appear in this log - the pipeline's own
`set +x` around that section (see jenkins/ci/Jenkinsfile) keeps them out of
the console entirely, confirmed by grepping this exact file for token
patterns before publishing it here.
==========================================================================
